Use when: creating PRs in discord-bot-cli, handling review feedback, polling CI status, or the user says "create PR", "review comments", "address feedback", "resolve threads". Renamed from `pr-review` in steward 0.7.0; rebased on devex in 0.12.0. ([agentculture/discord-bot-cli](https://github.com/agentculture/discord-bot-cli/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cicd) · ★1)
